# Every code point below U+110000 where ferrousli's wide character
# functions, on musl 1.2.5's Unicode 12.1.0 tables, disagree with the host
# glibc's C.UTF-8. Written by the unit test in src/wctype.rs and
# tools/unicode-differences.py; the test compares the runs and ignores
# everything after `#`.
#
# A line is a function, a run of code points, its length, and what each
# library returns: 0 or 1 for a class, the offset from the character for a
# case mapping, the width for wcwidth. Its annotation counts the run's code
# points by the Unicode version that assigned them (Perl's UCD 15.0.0,
# Python's 16.0.0); "after 16.0.0" means glibc knows a
# character neither does, and "unassigned" means no one does.
#
# Code points assigned after Unicode 12.1 differ because glibc's data is
# newer. Those assigned by 12.1 differ by definition, or because Unicode
# changed their properties since.
#
# function    differ   newer  by 12.1  unassigned
# iswalnum     23674   19613       40        4021
# iswalpha     23674   19613       40        4021
# iswblank        13       0       13           0
# iswcntrl         3       0        3           0
# iswgraph    814700       0       36      814664
# iswlower      1217     255      962           0
# iswprint    814699       0       35      814664
# iswpunct    139452    1605   137847           0
# iswspace         2       0        2           0
# iswupper       673      98      575           0
# towlower       124      98       26           0
# towupper       125      96       29           0
# wcwidth     818010    1037     2309      814664
#
# Where musl's definitions differ from glibc's, whatever the version:
#
# * iswprint, iswgraph and wcwidth: musl treats every unassigned scalar
#   value, surrogate and noncharacter such as U+FDD0..U+FDEF as printable, of
#   width 1. glibc treats them as unprintable, of width -1.
# * wcwidth: symbols such as the hexagrams U+4DC0..U+4DFF and U+1D300..U+1D356
#   became wide after Unicode 12.1; musl's tables still give them width 1.
# * iswpunct: glibc's punct is every graphic character that is not
#   alphanumeric, so it includes the private-use characters U+E000..U+F8FF
#   and planes 15 and 16, the tags and the variation selectors. musl's table
#   is punctuation and symbols only, and holds the combining letters
#   U+0363..U+036F and U+1DD3..U+1DE6, which glibc calls alphabetic.
# * iswalpha and iswalnum: besides those, musl counts all of U+20000..U+2FFFD
#   as alphabetic, assigned or not.
# * iswlower and iswupper: musl means "has a mapping to the other case".
#   glibc means the Lowercase and Uppercase properties, which include letters
#   without one: U+00AA, U+0138, the phonetic letters from U+1D00, the
#   mathematical letters and the circled letters.
# * towlower and towupper: musl maps only letters, so not the circled letters
#   U+24B6..U+24E9, and it maps U+00DF to U+1E9E, which glibc does not.
# * iswspace and iswblank: musl excludes U+1680 and counts U+0085 as space;
#   its iswblank is only space and tab.
# * iswcntrl: musl includes U+FFF9..U+FFFB.

iswalnum U+0363..U+036F 13 musl +0 glibc +1  # 3.2: 13
iswalnum U+0870..U+0887 24 musl +0 glibc +1  # 14.0: 24
iswalnum U+0889..U+088F 7 musl +0 glibc +1  # 14.0: 6, after 16.0.0: 1
iswalnum U+0897..U+0897 1 musl +0 glibc +1  # after 15.0.0: 1
iswalnum U+08B5..U+08B5 1 musl +0 glibc +1  # 14.0: 1
